The Danish Agency treats DP Course Results of at least 18 points, with at least grade 3 in all six subjects and at least three of them at HL, as equivalent to a qualifying examination for a Danish university bachelor once you have added two successive raises in the level of upper-secondary subjects, at least one of them to A level, taken as hf-enkeltfag or as a self-study candidate. If you already hold an A-level subject other than Danish, two raises to B level are enough. Retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma is the other published route.

      ✗ This asks for the full IB Diploma, and Course Results on their own do not satisfy it. It does not close this one to you. The Danish Agency treats DP Course Results of at least 18 points, with at least grade 3 in all six subjects and at least three of them at HL, as equivalent to a qualifying examination for a Danish university bachelor once you have added two successive raises in the level of upper-secondary subjects, at least one of them to A level, taken as hf-enkeltfag or as a self-study candidate. If you already hold an A-level subject other than Danish, two raises to B level are enough. Retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma is the other published route.

[near] Applied Data Science and Artificial Intelligence (BUas Breda)
      ✗ This asks for the full IB Diploma, and Course Results on their own do not satisfy it. It does not close this one to you, and Breda is the only institution in this catalogue whose own rules open a door before you turn 21. BUas admits on a havo- or vwo-equivalent diploma, and its list of international qualifications gives the IB exactly two entries — the Career-related Certificate against havo, the IB Diploma against vwo — with nothing for Course Results. What it publishes instead is the 21+ assessment, run under article 7.29 of the Dutch Higher Education Act: a computer-based capability test of your mathematical, linguistic and spatial skills, 150 euro, sat on 4 February and 15 April 2027, passed at 5 of 9 points, valid for two years and only at BUas. You apply for the programme through Studielink first — by 1 May at the latest — then answer the extra questions in My Online Application or email studentoffice@buas.nl saying you do not meet the entry requirements; an English test is still required on top of it, and a failed assessment cannot be resat the same year. Normally you must be 21 when the programme starts. The exception is the part worth writing to them about: BUas's own Regeling aanmelding en inschrijving says the Executive Board "kan besluiten de studiekiezer, die in het bezit is van een buiten Nederland afgegeven getuigschrift, deel te laten nemen aan het toelatingsonderzoek, ook indien de leeftijd van 21 jaar nog niet bereikt is" — it may let the holder of a qualification issued outside the Netherlands sit the entrance assessment before 21, and the same applies in special cases where no diploma can be produced. DP Course Results are a document issued outside the Netherlands and Nuffic calls them a transcript rather than a diploma. Nothing says BUas will use that discretion for you — the verb is "kan besluiten" and the decision is the Board's — so put the question to studentoffice@buas.nl in writing and early, name the article, and keep a plan that does not depend on the answer. One further route is national rather than this institution's, and it is the only one that produces the qualification itself instead of an exemption from it: the Dutch staatsexamen vwo. Anyone who registers may sit vwo subjects one at a time with DUO — registration for the 2027 examinations runs from 15 October to 31 December 2026, each subject costs 144 euro with a ceiling of 720 euro a year, and the certificates stay valid for ten years, so the package can be built up and exchanged for a vwo diploma at the end. No age limit applies, which is what makes it different from every colloquium doctum above, and Dutch law lets the College voor Toetsen en Examens grant you ontheffing from individual subjects on the strength of International Baccalaureate results — the one place in Dutch education law where the IB is named. The bill is linguistic: by statute the examinations are sat in Dutch.
      ? Documented English proficiency — the exemption is for full IB Diploma holders, so with Course Results you would need to meet it directly. Check the official page.
      Possible with action
      Restricted admission.

      ✗ This asks for the full IB Diploma, and Course Results on their own do not satisfy it. Erasmus publishes a colloquium doctum and does not publish it for this programme. Under article 7.29 of the Dutch Higher Education Act it admits applicants who are 21 or older before 1 September and hold no qualifying prior education, on the strength of VWO partial certificates — but the twelve programmes it lists are Business Administration, Criminology, Dutch Law, Health Sciences, Liberal Arts & Sciences, Medicine, Pedagogical Sciences, Philosophy, Psychology, Public Administration, Sociology and Tax Law, and the International Bachelor Economics and Business Economics is not among them. Where the route is open it is self-service: Erasmus runs no entrance examinations, so you obtain the VWO subject certificates yourself through the Dutch State Examinations (registration closes 31 December before the year you want to start), OMPT, CCVX or Boswell Beta, register through Studielink by 15 January for a numerus fixus programme and submit the certificates by 1 August — and for the programmes whose requirements are published, the set includes a VWO partial certificate in Dutch at 7.0. Erasmus separately states that foundation year programmes are not accepted for entry to its bachelor's degrees and that homologation of a foreign diploma is not valid for admission. So for this programme, ask the Central Admissions Office in writing whether a colloquium doctum can be opened for it, and plan on retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma. You apply by selecting the highest level of previous education you have completed in Studielink, then uploading a personal statement in the MyApplication portal saying you are applying on the basis of the Colloquium Doctum, together with your transcripts, and you ask dacs-admissions@maastrichtuniversity.nl what the assessment consists of — the page does not publish its content, its date or its cost.       ✗ This asks for the full IB Diploma, and Course Results on their own do not satisfy it. It does not close this one to you outright, but the only route TU Delft publishes is narrow, slow, and not on the pages an IB applicant is sent to. TU Delft admits on diplomas: it states that it "does not accept separate Dutch language certificates, English language certificates, or individual VWO subject certificates as a basis for admission", and individual VWO subject certificates — vwo-deelcertificaten — are exactly what Nuffic compares DP Course Results to. The published alternative is the colloquium doctum, which TU Delft documents only on its Dutch-diploma pages, and those pages send every IB holder away to the international-diploma route where the word never appears. To use it you must be 21 or older on the day the programme starts, show sufficient proficiency in Dutch — TU Delft states this applies to its English-taught programmes too — and meet the English requirement as well. No syllabus, deadline or fee is published: you arrange it by emailing contactcentre-esa@tudelft.nl with 'Colloquium Doctum' in the subject. Two things on the site look like routes and are not. The Dutch foundation-year programmes TU Delft lists sit inside a section that applies only to recognised refugees holding a Dutch residence permit, and TU Delft offers no foundation year of its own. The hbo propedeuse and associate degree route needs you to be admitted to a Dutch hogeschool first, and that asks for a havo, vwo or mbo-4 diploma, which brings you back to where you started. Retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma is the only route that reaches this programme at eighteen. To use it you must be 21 or older on the day the programme starts, show sufficient proficiency in Dutch — TU Delft states this applies to its English-taught programmes too — and meet the English requirement as well. No syllabus, deadline or fee is published: you arrange it by emailing contactcentre-esa@tudelft.nl with 'Colloquium Doctum' in the subject. Two things on the site look like routes and are not. The Dutch foundation-year programmes TU Delft lists sit inside a section that applies only to recognised refugees holding a Dutch residence permit, and TU Delft offers no foundation year of its own. The hbo propedeuse and associate degree route needs you to be admitted to a Dutch hogeschool first, and that asks for a havo, vwo or mbo-4 diploma, which brings you back to where you started. Retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma is the only route that reaches this programme at eighteen. One further route is national rather than this institution's, and it is the only one that produces the qualification itself instead of an exemption from it: the Dutch staatsexamen vwo. Anyone who registers may sit vwo subjects one at a time with DUO — registration for the 2027 examinations runs from 15 October to 31 December 2026, each subject costs 144 euro with a ceiling of 720 euro a year, and the certificates stay valid for ten years, so the package can be built up and exchanged for a vwo diploma at the end. No age limit applies, which is what makes it different from every colloquium doctum above, and Dutch law lets the College voor Toetsen en Examens grant you ontheffing from individual subjects on the strength of International Baccalaureate results — the one place in Dutch education law where the IB is named. The bill is linguistic: by statute the examinations are sat in Dutch.
      ? Documented English proficiency — the exemption is for full IB Diploma holders, so with Course Results you would need to meet it directly. Check the official page.
      Possible with action
      Restricted admission.

      ✗ This asks for the full IB Diploma, and Course Results on their own do not satisfy it. It does not close this one to you, and Twente publishes the alternative in more detail than any other Dutch institution here. Twente names IB certificates once, and only "in combination with another high school diploma", so Course Results standing alone are not directly admissible. What it publishes instead is the Colloquium Doctum (toelatingsonderzoek), which it runs under articles 7.25, 7.28 and 7.29 of the Dutch Higher Education Act. You must be at least 21 on 1 September of the year you start. Applications open on 1 October through Studielink like anybody else's: you file a normal enrolment request before the programme's deadline, then complete the Osiris questionnaire, upload whatever diplomas you do hold and a blank PDF stating that you are applying via a full Colloquium Doctum. You then have to pass the VWO-level subjects Twente lists for this programme — VWO English, VWO Mathematics B and VWO Physics — with certificates from CCVX, James Boswell or the Dutch Official State Examination, all before that programme's deadline. There is no blanket Dutch-language requirement: VWO Dutch appears in Twente's table only against its Dutch-taught programmes, and not against this one. Twente also publishes two slower routes: an hbo propedeuse, the first 60 ECTS of a bachelor at a Dutch university of applied sciences, NVAO-accredited and relevant to the programme, which it says does not guarantee admission; and a foundation year, accepted only where the resulting qualification is nationally accredited in the country it was taken in, assessed case by case. All of these wait for your 21st birthday. Retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma is the route that reaches this at eighteen. One further route is national rather than this institution's, and it is the only one that produces the qualification itself instead of an exemption from it: the Dutch staatsexamen vwo. Anyone who registers may sit vwo subjects one at a time with DUO — registration for the 2027 examinations runs from 15 October to 31 December 2026, each subject costs 144 euro with a ceiling of 720 euro a year, and the certificates stay valid for ten years, so the package can be built up and exchanged for a vwo diploma at the end. No age limit applies, which is what makes it different from every colloquium doctum above, and Dutch law lets the College voor Toetsen en Examens grant you ontheffing from individual subjects on the strength of International Baccalaureate results — the one place in Dutch education law where the IB is named. The bill is linguistic: by statute the examinations are sat in Dutch.

[no] Nanobiology (TU Delft Delft)
      ✓ Your Mathematics: Analysis and Approaches HL meets HL. This requirement is published in IB terms, so nothing is converted.
      ✓ Your Physics HL meets HL. This requirement is published in IB terms, so nothing is converted.
      ✓ Your Chemistry SL meets SL. This requirement is published in IB terms, so nothing is converted.
      ✗ This asks for the full IB Diploma, and Course Results on their own do not satisfy it. It does not close this one to you outright, but the only route TU Delft publishes is narrow, slow, and not on the pages an IB applicant is sent to. TU Delft admits on diplomas: it states that it "does not accept separate Dutch language certificates, English language certificates, or individual VWO subject certificates as a basis for admission", and individual VWO subject certificates — vwo-deelcertificaten — are exactly what Nuffic compares DP Course Results to. The published alternative is the colloquium doctum, which TU Delft documents only on its Dutch-diploma pages, and those pages send every IB holder away to the international-diploma route where the word never appears. To use it you must be 21 or older on the day the programme starts, show sufficient proficiency in Dutch — TU Delft states this applies to its English-taught programmes too — and meet the English requirement as well. No syllabus, deadline or fee is published: you arrange it by emailing contactcentre-esa@tudelft.nl with 'Colloquium Doctum' in the subject. Two things on the site look like routes and are not. The Dutch foundation-year programmes TU Delft lists sit inside a section that applies only to recognised refugees holding a Dutch residence permit, and TU Delft offers no foundation year of its own. The hbo propedeuse and associate degree route needs you to be admitted to a Dutch hogeschool first, and that asks for a havo, vwo or mbo-4 diploma, which brings you back to where you started. Retaking to be awarded the full IB Diploma is the only route that reaches this programme at eighteen. One further route is national rather than this institution's, and it is the only one that produces the qualification itself instead of an exemption from it: the Dutch staatsexamen vwo. Anyone who registers may sit vwo subjects one at a time with DUO — registration for the 2027 examinations runs from 15 October to 31 December 2026, each subject costs 144 euro with a ceiling of 720 euro a year, and the certificates stay valid for ten years, so the package can be built up and exchanged for a vwo diploma at the end. No age limit applies, which is what makes it different from every colloquium doctum above, and Dutch law lets the College voor Toetsen en Examens grant you ontheffing from individual subjects on the strength of International Baccalaureate results — the one place in Dutch education law where the IB is named. The bill is linguistic: by statute the examinations are sat in Dutch.
      ✗ You do not have Biology at any level.
      ? Documented English proficiency — the exemption is for full IB Diploma holders, so with Course Results you would need to meet it directly. Check the official page.

      Restricted admission.
